  394 -- |'ZabrSmileSection'\'s evaluation-tag axis (Andreasen\/Huge 2011): 'ZabrShortMaturityLognormal'\/
  395 -- 'ZabrShortMaturityNormal' are closed-form short-maturity expansions (fast, less accurate away
  396 -- from short maturities); 'ZabrLocalVolatility' and 'ZabrFullFd' solve a finite-difference PDE
  397 -- (slower, more accurate). Local to this binding -- not shared with any other cross-cutting enum.
  398 data ZabrEvaluation = ZabrShortMaturityLognormal
  399                     | ZabrShortMaturityNormal
  400                     | ZabrLocalVolatility
  401                     | ZabrFullFd
  402   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  403 instance Enum ZabrEvaluation where
  404   succ ZabrShortMaturityLognormal = ZabrShortMaturityNormal
  405   succ ZabrShortMaturityNormal = ZabrLocalVolatility
  406   succ ZabrLocalVolatility = ZabrFullFd
  407   succ ZabrFullFd = error "ZabrEvaluation.succ: ZabrFullFd has no successor"
  408 
  409   pred ZabrShortMaturityNormal = ZabrShortMaturityLognormal
  410   pred ZabrLocalVolatility = ZabrShortMaturityNormal
  411   pred ZabrFullFd = ZabrLocalVolatility
  412   pred ZabrShortMaturityLognormal = error "ZabrEvaluation.pred: ZabrShortMaturityLognormal has no predecessor"
  413 
  414   enumFromTo from to = go from
  415     where
  416       end = fromEnum to
  417       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  418                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  419                  EQ -> [v]
  420                  GT -> []
  421 
  422   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from ZabrFullFd
  423 
  424   fromEnum ZabrShortMaturityLognormal = 0
  425   fromEnum ZabrShortMaturityNormal = 1
  426   fromEnum ZabrLocalVolatility = 2
  427   fromEnum ZabrFullFd = 3
  428 
  429   toEnum 0 = ZabrShortMaturityLognormal
  430   toEnum 1 = ZabrShortMaturityNormal
  431   toEnum 2 = ZabrLocalVolatility
  432   toEnum 3 = ZabrFullFd
  433   toEnum unmatched = error ("ZabrEvaluation.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)

  512 -- |A local vol surface derived from a Black vol surface via Dupire's formula (Gatheral's
  513 -- implementation).
  514 localVolSurface :: (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^riskFreeTS
  515  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^dividendTS
  516  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^underlying
  517  -> IO ((LocalVolTermStructure))
  518 localVolSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  519   withBlackVolTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  520   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  521   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  522   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  523   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  524   localVolSurface'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  525   peekLocalVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
  526   errorCheck  a5'>>
  527   return (res')
  528 
  529 
  530 
  531 -- |as 'localVolSurface', but a local vol calculation that would otherwise throw returns
  532 -- @illegalLocalVolOverwrite@ instead
  533 noExceptLocalVolSurface :: (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^riskFreeTS
  534  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^dividendTS
  535  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^underlying
  536  -> (Double) -- ^illegalLocalVolOverwrite
  537  -> IO ((LocalVolTermStructure))
  538 noExceptLocalVolSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
  539   withBlackVolTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  540   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  541   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  542   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  543   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
  544   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
  545   noExceptLocalVolSurface'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
  546   peekLocalVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
  547   errorCheck  a6'>>
  548   return (res')
  549

  552 -- |a local vol surface fed directly from a matrix of local vols (rather than derived from a
  553 -- Black vol surface, as 'localVolSurface' is) -- one flat strike grid shared across all dates,
  554 -- same shape as 'blackVarianceSurface'.
  555 fixedLocalVolSurface :: Day -> [Day] -- ^dates
  556   -> [Double] -- ^strikes
  557   -> RealMatrix -- ^localVolMatrix
  558   -> DayCounter
  559   -> FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation -- ^lowerExtrapolation
  560   -> FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation -- ^upperExtrapolation
  561   -> IO LocalVolTermStructure
  562 fixedLocalVolSurface d ds s (RealMatrix mr mc md) = qlFixedLocalVolSurface d ds s mr mc md
  563 qlFixedLocalVolSurface :: (Day) -> ([Day]) -> ([Double]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> (RealVector) -> (DayCounter) -> (FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation) -> (FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation) -> IO ((LocalVolTermStructure))
  564 qlFixedLocalVolSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  565   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
  566   withDayArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
  567   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
  568   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
  569   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
  570   withRealVectorRaw a6 $ \a6' -> 
  571   withDayCounter a7 $ \a7' -> 
  572   let {a8' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a8} in 
  573   let {a9' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a9} in 
  574   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  575   qlFixedLocalVolSurface'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  576   peekLocalVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
  577   errorCheck  a10'>>
  578   return (res')

  582 -- |A local-volatility surface whose node values are model parameters. Each date is paired with
  583 -- its non-empty strike row, so this genuinely ragged grid does not masquerade as a rectangular
  584 -- 'Matrix'. Use 'gridModelLocalVolSurfaceAsCalibratedModel' to access QuantLib's generic
  585 -- calibrated-model operations.
  586 gridModelLocalVolSurface :: Day -> NonEmpty (Day, NonEmpty Double) -> DayCounter
  587   -> FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation -> FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation
  588   -> IO GridModelLocalVolSurface
  589 gridModelLocalVolSurface d nodes = qlGridModelLocalVolSurface d dates rowSizes values
  590   where
  591     (dates, rows) = unzip (toList nodes)
  592     rowLists = map toList rows
  593     rowSizes = map (fromIntegral . length) rowLists
  594     values = concat rowLists
  595 qlGridModelLocalVolSurface :: (Day) -> ([Day]) -> ([Word]) -> ([Double]) -> (DayCounter) -> (FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation) -> (FixedLocalVolSurfaceExtrapolation) -> IO ((GridModelLocalVolSurface))
  596 qlGridModelLocalVolSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
  597   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
  598   withDayArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
  599   withIntArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
  600   withDoubleArrayRaw a4 $ \a4' -> 
  601   withDayCounter a5 $ \a5' -> 
  602   let {a6' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a6} in 
  603   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
  604   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
  605   qlGridModelLocalVolSurface'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
  606   peekGridModelLocalVolSurface res >>= \res' ->
  607   errorCheck  a8'>>
  608   return (res')

  614 gridModelLocalVolSurfaceAsCalibratedModel :: (GridModelLocalVolSurface) -- ^surface
  615  -> IO ((CalibratedModel))
  616 gridModelLocalVolSurfaceAsCalibratedModel a1 =

  626 -- |A Black implied-volatility surface computed from a Heston model. The integration argument is
  627 -- the order of QuantLib's Gauss-Laguerre quadrature; the supplied complex-log formula controls
  628 -- the characteristic-function representation.
  629 hestonBlackVolSurface :: (GenHestonModel hm) -- ^model
  630  -> (ComplexLogFormula) -- ^complexLogFormula
  631  -> (Word) -- ^integrationOrder
  632  -> IO ((BlackVolTermStructure))
  633 hestonBlackVolSurface a1 a2 a3 =
  634   withHestonModel a1 $ \a1' -> 
  635   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
  636   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
  637   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  638   hestonBlackVolSurface'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  639   peekBlackVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
  640   errorCheck  a4'>>
  641   return (res')

  645 -- |Interpolation choice for Andreasen-Huge local-volatility calibration.
  646 -- |Calibration choice for Andreasen-Huge local-volatility calibration.
  647 andreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl :: NonEmpty (VanillaOption, GenQuote q) -> GenQuote q
  648   -> GenYieldTermStructure y1 -> GenYieldTermStructure y2
  649   -> AndreasenHugeInterpolationType -> AndreasenHugeCalibrationType -> Word
  650   -> Maybe Double -> Maybe Double -> OptimizationMethod -> EndCriteria
  651   -> IO AndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl
  652 andreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl xs spot r q = qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl os qs spot r q
  653   where (os, qs) = unzip (toList xs)
  654 qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl :: ([VanillaOption]) -> ([GenQuote q1]) -> (GenQuote q2) -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -> (AndreasenHugeInterpolationType) -> (AndreasenHugeCalibrationType) -> (Word) -> (Maybe Double) -> (Maybe Double) -> (OptimizationMethod) -> (EndCriteria) -> IO ((AndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl))
  655 qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 =
  656   withVanillaOptionArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
  657   withQuoteArrayRaw a2 $ \a2' -> 
  658   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  659   withYieldTermStructure a4 $ \a4' -> 
  660   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  661   let {a6' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a6} in 
  662   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
  663   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in 
  664   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
  665   let {a10' = fromMaybeDouble a10} in 
  666   withOptimizationMethod a11 $ \a11' -> 
  667   withEndCriteria a12 $ \a12' -> 
  668   preErrorCheck $ \a13' -> 
  669   qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' >>= \res ->
  670   peekAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl res >>= \res' ->
  671   errorCheck  a13'>>
  672   return (res')
  673

  676 -- |Minimum, maximum, and average calibration errors, expressed in volatility units.
  677 andreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError :: AndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl -> IO (Double, Double, Double)
  678 andreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError x = do
  679   [mn, mx, av] <- qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError x
  680   pure (mn, mx, av)
  681 qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError :: (AndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl) -> IO (([Double]))
  682 qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError a1 =
  683   withAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterpl a1 $ \a1' -> 
  684   preArray $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
  685   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
  686   qlAndreasenHugeVolatilityInterplCalibrationError'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3' >>
  687   peekDoubleArray  a2'1  a2'2>>= \a2'' -> 
  688   errorCheck  a3'>>
  689   return (a2'')

  802   peekOptionletVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
  803   errorCheck  a8'>>
  804   return (res')

  828 -- |Optionlet volatility curve interpolating the supplied caplet-volatility nodes by variance.
  829 -- Each node pairs an option date with its volatility, so mismatched date/value inputs are
  830 -- unrepresentable.
  831 capletVarianceCurve :: Day -> NonEmpty (Day, Double) -> DayCounter -> VolatilityType -> Double
  832   -> IO OptionletVolatilityStructure
  833 capletVarianceCurve referenceDate nodes = qlCapletVarianceCurve referenceDate dates vols
  834   where (dates, vols) = unzip (toList nodes)
  835 qlCapletVarianceCurve :: (Day) -- ^referenceDate
  836  -> ([Day]) -- ^dates
  837  -> ([Double]) -- ^capletVolCurve
  838  -> (DayCounter) -- ^dayCounter
  839  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^type
  840  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
  841  -> IO ((OptionletVolatilityStructure))
  842 qlCapletVarianceCurve a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
  843   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
  844   withDayArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
  845   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
  846   withDayCounter a4 $ \a4' -> 
  847   let {a5' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a5} in 
  848   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
  849   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
  850   qlCapletVarianceCurve'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
  851   peekOptionletVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
  852   errorCheck  a7'>>
  853   return (res')
  854

  857 -- |Strips a 'CapFloorTermVolSurface' (quoted cap\/floor term vols) into caplet\/floorlet vols via
  858 -- 'OptionletStripper1', immediately wrapping the result behind 'StrippedOptionletAdapter' in one
  859 -- step -- 'OptionletStripper1' itself is never exposed as a Haskell type, since none of its own
  860 -- getters (capFloorPrices\/capletVols\/etc.) are needed beyond feeding the adapter, per the "bind
  861 -- few inspectors" rule.
  862 optionletStripper1 :: (CapFloorTermVolSurface) -> (GenIborIndex ibor) -> (Maybe Double) -- ^switchStrikes
  863  -> (Double) -- ^accuracy
  864  -> (Word) -- ^maxIter
  865  -> (Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y)) -- ^discount
  866  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^type
  867  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
  868  -> (Bool) -- ^dontThrow
  869  -> (Maybe (Word, TimeUnit)) -- ^optionletFrequency
  870  -> IO ((OptionletVolatilityStructure))
  871 optionletStripper1 a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =
  872   withCapFloorTermVolSurface a1 $ \a1' -> 
  873   withIborIndex a2 $ \a2' -> 
  874   let {a3' = fromMaybeDouble a3} in 
  875   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
  876   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
  877   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  878   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
  879   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  880   let {a9' = C2HSImp.fromBool a9} in 
  881   let {(a10'1, a10'2) = fromMaybeEnumQuantity a10} in 
  882   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
  883   optionletStripper1'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10'1  a10'2 a11' >>= \res ->
  884   peekOptionletVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
  885   errorCheck  a11'>>
  886   return (res')
  887

  890 -- |Extends an 'OptionletStripper1' (built internally, never exposed as its own Haskell type --
  891 -- same fusion as 'optionletStripper1') by reconciling it against an ATM 'CapFloorTermVolCurve',
  892 -- producing forward-forward optionlet vols. Unlike 'optionletStripper1', this keeps its own
  893 -- concrete 'OptionletStripper2' object around, since it has real diagnostic getters
  894 -- ('optionletStripper2AtmCapFloorStrikes'\/'optionletStripper2AtmCapFloorPrices'\/
  895 -- 'optionletStripper2SpreadsVol') not reachable through the generic
  896 -- 'OptionletVolatilityStructure' interface. Use 'optionletStripper2AsOptionletVolatilityStructure'
  897 -- to pass one into anything expecting the generic interface.
  898 optionletStripper2 :: (CapFloorTermVolSurface) -> (GenIborIndex ibor) -> (Maybe Double) -- ^switchStrikes
  899  -> (Double) -- ^accuracy
  900  -> (Word) -- ^maxIter
  901  -> (Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y)) -- ^discount
  902  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^type
  903  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
  904  -> (Bool) -- ^dontThrow
  905  -> (Maybe (Word, TimeUnit)) -- ^optionletFrequency
  906  -> (CapFloorTermVolCurve) -- ^atmCapFloorTermVolCurve
  907  -> IO ((OptionletStripper2))
  908 optionletStripper2 a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
  909   withCapFloorTermVolSurface a1 $ \a1' -> 
  910   withIborIndex a2 $ \a2' -> 
  911   let {a3' = fromMaybeDouble a3} in 
  912   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
  913   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
  914   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  915   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
  916   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  917   let {a9' = C2HSImp.fromBool a9} in 
  918   let {(a10'1, a10'2) = fromMaybeEnumQuantity a10} in 
  919   withCapFloorTermVolCurve a11 $ \a11' -> 
  920   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
  921   optionletStripper2'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10'1  a10'2 a11' a12' >>= \res ->
  922   peekOptionletStripper2 res >>= \res' ->
  923   errorCheck  a12'>>
  924   return (res')
  925

  928 -- |Fresh construction (a 'StrippedOptionletAdapter' wrapping the 'OptionletStripper2' itself),
  929 -- never a cast -- same idiom as 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection'.
  930 optionletStripper2AsOptionletVolatilityStructure :: (OptionletStripper2) -> IO ((OptionletVolatilityStructure))
  931 optionletStripper2AsOptionletVolatilityStructure a1 =
  932   withOptionletStripper2 a1 $ \a1' -> 
  933   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
  934   optionletStripper2AsOptionletVolatilityStructure'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
  935   peekOptionletVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
  936   errorCheck  a2'>>
  937   return (res')

  977 -- |An optionlet vol surface behind a relinkable handle. The result /is/ an
  978 -- 'OptionletVolatilityStructure': pass it anywhere one is expected and everything built on it
  979 -- keeps tracking whatever the handle currently points at, so a later 'linkOptionletVolTo'
  980 -- reprices already-constructed instruments without rebuilding them. Mirrors
  981 -- 'relinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure'.
  982 relinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure :: (Maybe (GenOptionletVolatilityStructure ov)) -> IO ((RelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure))
  983 relinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure a1 =
  984   withMaybeOptionletVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  985   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
  986   relinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
  987   peekRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
  988   errorCheck  a2'>>
  989   return (res')
  990 
  991 
  992 
  993 -- |Point a relinkable optionlet vol handle at a different surface. Everything already built on
  994 -- the handle reprices against the new surface, with no engine rebuilt. Named distinctly from
  995 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.linkTo'\/'linkBlackVolTo'\/'linkSwaptionVolTo' for the same
  996 -- reason as those: all four relinkable vol types live in this one module.
  997 linkOptionletVolTo :: (RelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure) -> (GenOptionletVolatilityStructure ov) -> IO ()
  998 linkOptionletVolTo a1 a2 =
  999   withRelinkableOptionletVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1000   withOptionletVolatilityStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1001   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 1002   linkOptionletVolTo'_ a1' a2' a3' >>
 1003   errorCheck  a3'>>
 1004   return ()

 1008 -- |A constant Black volatility, no time-strike dependence -- floating reference date, floating
 1009 -- market data
 1010 blackConstantVol' :: (Word) -> (Calendar) -> (GenQuote q) -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((BlackVolTermStructure))
 1011 blackConstantVol' a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 1012   let {a1' = fromIntegral a1} in 
 1013   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1014   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1015   withDayCounter a4 $ \a4' -> 
 1016   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 1017   blackConstantVol''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 1018   peekBlackVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
 1019   errorCheck  a5'>>
 1020   return (res')

 1039 -- |A Black vol surface behind a relinkable handle. The result /is/ a 'BlackVolTermStructure':
 1040 -- pass it anywhere one is expected and everything built on it keeps tracking whatever the
 1041 -- handle currently points at, so a later 'linkBlackVolTo' reprices already-constructed
 1042 -- instruments without rebuilding them. Mirrors
 1043 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.relinkableYieldTermStructure'.
 1044 relinkableBlackVolTermStructure :: (Maybe (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv)) -> IO ((RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure))
 1045 relinkableBlackVolTermStructure a1 =
 1046   withMaybeBlackVolTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1047   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
 1048   relinkableBlackVolTermStructure'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
 1049   peekRelinkableBlackVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
 1050   errorCheck  a2'>>
 1051   return (res')
 1052

 1055 -- |Point a relinkable Black vol handle at a different surface. Everything already built on the
 1056 -- handle reprices against the new surface, with no engine rebuilt. Mirrors
 1057 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.linkTo' -- see its haddock for why this mutator is justified.
 1058 linkBlackVolTo :: (RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure) -> (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -> IO ()
 1059 linkBlackVolTo a1 a2 =
 1060   withRelinkableBlackVolTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1061   withBlackVolTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1062   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 1063   linkBlackVolTo'_ a1' a2' a3' >>
 1064   errorCheck  a3'>>
 1065   return ()

 1069 -- |fixed reference date, floating market data
 1070 constantSwaptionVolatility' :: (Day) -> (Calendar) -> (BusinessDayConvention) -> (GenQuote q) -> (DayCounter) -> (VolatilityType) -- ^type
 1071  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1072  -> IO ((SwaptionVolatilityStructure))
 1073 constantSwaptionVolatility' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 1074   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1075   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1076   let {a3' = fromEnumC a3} in 
 1077   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
 1078   withDayCounter a5 $ \a5' -> 
 1079   let {a6' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a6} in 
 1080   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1081   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 1082   constantSwaptionVolatility''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 1083   peekSwaptionVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
 1084   errorCheck  a8'>>
 1085   return (res')

 1363 -- |a smile section built directly from SABR parameters (Hagan et al. 2002), rather than
 1364 -- interpolated from a 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure'
 1365 sabrSmileSection :: (Double) -- ^timeToExpiry
 1366  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1367  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1368  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1369  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1370  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1371  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1372  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 1373  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1374 sabrSmileSection a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 1375   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 1376   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1377   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1378   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1379   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1380   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1381   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1382   let {a8' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a8} in 
 1383   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 1384   sabrSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 1385   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1386   errorCheck  a9'>>
 1387   return (res')
 1388

 1390 
 1391 -- |as 'sabrSmileSection', but the time to expiry is derived from a date, reference date and day
 1392 -- counter rather than given directly
 1393 sabrSmileSection' :: (Day) -- ^optionDate
 1394  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1395  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1396  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1397  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1398  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1399  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^referenceDate
 1400  -> (DayCounter) -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1401  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 1402  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1403 sabrSmileSection' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =
 1404   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1405   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1406   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1407   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1408   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1409   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1410   withMaybeDay a7 $ \a7' -> 
 1411   withDayCounter a8 $ \a8' -> 
 1412   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 1413   let {a10' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a10} in 
 1414   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
 1415   sabrSmileSection''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' >>= \res ->
 1416   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1417   errorCheck  a11'>>
 1418   return (res')

 1422 -- |an arbitrage-free SABR smile section (Doust's approach via 'NoArbSabrSmileSection'), built
 1423 -- directly from SABR parameters like 'sabrSmileSection' but guaranteeing a proper terminal density
 1424 noArbSabrSmileSection :: (Double) -- ^timeToExpiry
 1425  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1426  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1427  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1428  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1429  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1430  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1431  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 1432  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1433 noArbSabrSmileSection a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 1434   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 1435   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1436   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1437   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1438   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1439   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1440   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1441   let {a8' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a8} in 
 1442   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 1443   noArbSabrSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 1444   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1445   errorCheck  a9'>>
 1446   return (res')

 1454  -> (Double) -- ^alpha

 1520 -- |the (non-discounted-by-default) price of a European option with the given strike\/type,
 1521 -- for any 'SmileSection' (however it was constructed)
 1522 smileSectionOptionPrice :: (SmileSection) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 1523  -> (OptionType) -- ^type
 1524  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 1525  -> IO ((Double))
 1526 smileSectionOptionPrice a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 1527   withSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1528   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1529   let {a3' = fromEnumC a3} in 
 1530   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1531   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 1532   smileSectionOptionPrice'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 1533   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 1534   errorCheck  a5'>>
 1535   return (res')

 1539 -- |the price of a cash-or-nothing digital option with the given strike\/type, approximated via a
 1540 -- call\/put spread of width @gap@, for any 'SmileSection' (however it was constructed)
 1541 smileSectionDigitalOptionPrice :: (SmileSection) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 1542  -> (OptionType) -- ^type
 1543  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 1544  -> (Double) -- ^gap
 1545  -> IO ((Double))
 1546 smileSectionDigitalOptionPrice a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 1547   withSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1548   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1549   let {a3' = fromEnumC a3} in 
 1550   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1551   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1552   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 1553   smileSectionDigitalOptionPrice'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 1554   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 1555   errorCheck  a6'>>
 1556   return (res')
 1557

 1560 -- |the risk-neutral probability density at the given strike, approximated via a finite difference
 1561 -- of width @gap@ on 'smileSectionOptionPrice', for any 'SmileSection' (however it was constructed)
 1562 smileSectionDensity :: (SmileSection) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 1563  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 1564  -> (Double) -- ^gap
 1565  -> IO ((Double))
 1566 smileSectionDensity a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 1567   withSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1568   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1569   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1570   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1571   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 1572   smileSectionDensity'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 1573   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 1574   errorCheck  a5'>>
 1575   return (res')

 1579 -- |a flat-volatility smile section: 'volatility' returns @vol@ for every strike.
 1580 -- 'Nothing'\/'Nothing' reproduce upstream's own defaults for @referenceDate@\/@atmLevel@.
 1581 flatSmileSection :: (Day) -> (Double) -- ^vol
 1582  -> (DayCounter) -> (Maybe Day) -- ^referenceDate
 1583  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^atmLevel
 1584  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^type
 1585  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1586  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1587 flatSmileSection a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 1588   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1589   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1590   withDayCounter a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1591   withMaybeDay a4 $ \a4' -> 
 1592   let {a5' = fromMaybeDouble a5} in 
 1593   let {a6' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a6} in 
 1594   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1595   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 1596   flatSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 1597   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1598   errorCheck  a8'>>
 1599   return (res')

 1603 -- |a 'SmileSection' whose volatility at every strike is @source@'s plus @spread@ (which may
 1604 -- change over time, since it's a live 'GenQuote' rather than a fixed number)
 1605 spreadedSmileSection :: (SmileSection) -> (GenQuote q) -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1606 spreadedSmileSection a1 a2 =
 1607   withSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1608   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1609   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 1610   spreadedSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
 1611   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1612   errorCheck  a3'>>
 1613   return (res')

 1617 -- |@source@ re-anchored to a different ATM level ('Nothing' reproduces upstream's own default,
 1618 -- which recomputes the ATM level from @source@ itself). @source@'s volatility at every other
 1619 -- strike is unchanged -- use 'smileSectionAtmLevel' to observe what this changed.
 1620 atmSmileSection :: (SmileSection) -> (Maybe Double) -- ^atm
 1621  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1622 atmSmileSection a1 a2 =
 1623   withSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1624   let {a2' = fromMaybeDouble a2} in 
 1625   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 1626   atmSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
 1627   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1628   errorCheck  a3'>>
 1629   return (res')

 1633 -- |a Stochastic Volatility Inspired (SVI) smile section: total variance at
 1634 -- log-moneyness @k = log(strike\/forward)@ is @a + b*(rho*(k-m) + sqrt((k-m)^2 + sigma^2))@.
 1635 -- Direct evaluation, no calibration.
 1636 sviSmileSection :: (Day) -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1637  -> (Double) -- ^a
 1638  -> (Double) -- ^b
 1639  -> (Double) -- ^sigma
 1640  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1641  -> (Double) -- ^m
 1642  -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1643 sviSmileSection a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 1644   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1645   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1646   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1647   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1648   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1649   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1650   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1651   withDayCounter a8 $ \a8' -> 
 1652   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 1653   sviSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 1654   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1655   errorCheck  a9'>>
 1656   return (res')
 1657 
 1658 
 1659 
 1660 -- |a ZABR (Andreasen\/Huge 2011) smile section: direct evaluation (no calibration) of a SABR-like
 1661 -- model widened by a fifth parameter, @gamma@, that controls the backbone shape away from
 1662 -- @gamma = 1@ (which reduces exactly to Hagan\'s SABR). @evaluation@ selects how the price\/vol at
 1663 -- a strike is computed -- see 'ZabrEvaluation'. @moneyness@ is the strike grid (as multiples of
 1664 -- the forward) used only by 'ZabrLocalVolatility'\/'ZabrFullFd' to build their finite-difference
 1665 -- solve; an empty list reproduces upstream\'s own 21-point default grid, and it is ignored by the
 1666 -- two closed-form evaluation modes. @fdRefinement@ subdivides each grid interval for the FD
 1667 -- solve\'s accuracy\/speed tradeoff (upstream\'s own default is 5).
 1668 zabrSmileSection :: (ZabrEvaluation) -> (Double) -- ^timeToExpiry
 1669  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1670  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1671  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1672  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1673  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1674  -> (Double) -- ^gamma
 1675  -> ([Double]) -- ^moneyness
 1676  -> (Word) -- ^fdRefinement
 1677  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1678 zabrSmileSection a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =
 1679   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1680   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1681   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1682   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1683   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1684   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1685   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1686   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 1687   withDoubleArray a9 $ \(a9'1, a9'2) -> 
 1688   let {a10' = fromIntegral a10} in 
 1689   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
 1690   zabrSmileSection'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9'1  a9'2 a10' a11' >>= \res ->
 1691   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1692   errorCheck  a11'>>
 1693   return (res')
 1694 
 1695 
 1696 
 1697 -- |as 'zabrSmileSection', but the time to expiry is derived from a date and day counter rather
 1698 -- than given directly.
 1699 zabrSmileSection' :: (ZabrEvaluation) -> (Day) -- ^optionDate
 1700  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 1701  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1702  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1703  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1704  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1705  -> (Double) -- ^gamma
 1706  -> (DayCounter) -> ([Double]) -- ^moneyness
 1707  -> (Word) -- ^fdRefinement
 1708  -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1709 zabrSmileSection' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1710   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1711   withDay a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1712   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1713   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1714   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1715   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1716   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1717   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 1718   withDayCounter a9 $ \a9' -> 
 1719   withDoubleArray a10 $ \(a10'1, a10'2) -> 
 1720   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1721   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1722   zabrSmileSection''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10'1  a10'2 a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1723   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1724   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1725   return (res')
 1726 
 1727 
 1728 
 1729 -- |a smile section calibrated to a market smile (strikes/vols given directly, not as live
 1730 -- quotes -- calibration runs once, eagerly, at construction). alpha\/beta\/nu\/rho\/vegaWeighted
 1731 -- are the SABR calibration's initial guess and fixed\/free flags; 'sabrEndCriteria'\/
 1732 -- 'sabrOptimizationMethod' default to 'Nothing', which falls back to QuantLib's own internal
 1733 -- defaults -- a caller-supplied 'EndCriteria'\/'OptimizationMethod' is safe to pass here since
 1734 -- both are shared_ptr-boxed on the C++ side (see the qlaux.h comment above the
 1735 -- QlEndCriteria\/QlOptimizationMethod typedefs), so the copy this object's constructor keeps
 1736 -- survives regardless of when Haskell's own handle is collected.
 1737 sabrInterpolatedSmileSection :: Day -- ^optionDate
 1738   -> GenQuote q1 -- ^forward
 1739   -> NonEmpty (Double, GenQuote q3) -- ^strike/volatility quotes
 1740   -> Bool -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 1741   -> GenQuote q2 -- ^atmVolatility
 1742   -> Double -- ^alpha
 1743   -> Double -- ^beta
 1744   -> Double -- ^nu
 1745   -> Double -- ^rho
 1746   -> SabrInterpolatedSmileSectionOpts -> IO SabrInterpolatedSmileSection
 1747 sabrInterpolatedSmileSection optionDate forward strikeVols hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility
 1748   alpha beta nu rho opts = do
 1749   dc <- maybe (dayCounter Actual365FixedStandard) return (sabrDayCounter opts)
 1750   sabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ optionDate forward strikes hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility vols
 1751     alpha beta nu rho (sabrIsAlphaFixed opts) (sabrIsBetaFixed opts) (sabrIsNuFixed opts)
 1752     (sabrIsRhoFixed opts) (sabrVegaWeighted opts) (sabrEndCriteria opts) (sabrOptimizationMethod opts)
 1753     dc (sabrShift opts)
 1754   where (strikes, vols) = unzip (toList strikeVols)
 1755 
 1756 sabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ :: (Day) -> (GenQuote q1) -- ^forward
 1757  -> ([Double]) -- ^strikes
 1758  -> (Bool) -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 1759  -> (GenQuote q2) -- ^atmVolatility
 1760  -> ([GenQuote q3]) -- ^vols
 1761  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 1762  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 1763  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 1764  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1765  -> (Bool) -- ^isAlphaFixed
 1766  -> (Bool) -- ^isBetaFixed
 1767  -> (Bool) -- ^isNuFixed
 1768  -> (Bool) -- ^isRhoFixed
 1769  -> (Bool) -- ^vegaWeighted
 1770  -> (Maybe EndCriteria) -> (Maybe OptimizationMethod) -> (DayCounter) -> (Double) -- ^shift
 1771  -> IO ((SabrInterpolatedSmileSection))
 1772 sabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 a17 a18 a19 =
 1773   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1774   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1775   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 1776   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 1777   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
 1778   withQuoteArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 1779   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1780   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 1781   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 1782   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 1783   let {a11' = C2HSImp.fromBool a11} in 
 1784   let {a12' = C2HSImp.fromBool a12} in 
 1785   let {a13' = C2HSImp.fromBool a13} in 
 1786   let {a14' = C2HSImp.fromBool a14} in 
 1787   let {a15' = C2HSImp.fromBool a15} in 
 1788   withMaybeEndCriteria a16 $ \a16' -> 
 1789   withMaybeOptimizationMethod a17 $ \a17' -> 
 1790   withDayCounter a18 $ \a18' -> 
 1791   let {a19' = realToFrac a19} in 
 1792   preErrorCheck $ \a20' -> 
 1793   sabrInterpolatedSmileSection_'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' a18' a19' a20' >>= \res ->
 1794   peekSabrInterpolatedSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1795   errorCheck  a20'>>
 1796   return (res')
 1797

 1800 -- |upcast to the generic 'SmileSection' interface (e.g. for 'smileSectionVolatility'\/'smileSectionVariance').
 1801 -- A fresh-@shared_ptr@ upcast, always safe -- not the reverse (downcast) direction.
 1802 sabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection :: (SabrInterpolatedSmileSection) -> IO ((SmileSection))
 1803 sabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection a1 =
 1804   withSabrInterpolatedSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1805   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
 1806   sabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
 1807   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1808   errorCheck  a2'>>
 1809   return (res')

 1886 -- |the reason the SABR calibration's optimizer stopped

 1894   return (res')

 1897 
 1898 -- |a smile section calibrated to a market smile using Stochastic Volatility Inspired (SVI, Gatheral
 1899 -- 2004) parameterization: total variance at log-moneyness @k = log(strike\/forward)@ is
 1900 -- @a + b*(rho*(k-m) + sqrt((k-m)^2 + sigma^2))@. Strikes\/vols are given directly (not as live
 1901 -- quotes) -- calibration runs once, eagerly, at construction, same as 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSection'.
 1902 -- @a@\/@b@\/@sigma@\/@rho@\/@m@ are the SVI calibration's initial guess;
 1903 -- @aIsFixed@\/@bIsFixed@\/@sigmaIsFixed@\/@rhoIsFixed@\/@mIsFixed@ have no upstream default (unlike
 1904 -- SABR's fixed flags, which default to 'False') and so are always required here. Calibration
 1905 -- enforces, at construction: @b >= 0@, @|rho| < 1@, @sigma > 0@,
 1906 -- @a + b*sigma*sqrt(1-rho^2) >= 0@, and @b*(1+|rho|) <= 4@.
 1907 sviInterpolatedSmileSection :: Day -- ^optionDate
 1908   -> GenQuote q1 -- ^forward
 1909   -> NonEmpty (Double, GenQuote q3) -- ^strike/volatility quotes
 1910   -> Bool -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 1911   -> GenQuote q2 -- ^atmVolatility
 1912   -> Double -- ^a
 1913   -> Double -- ^b
 1914   -> Double -- ^sigma
 1915   -> Double -- ^rho
 1916   -> Double -- ^m
 1917   -> Bool -- ^aIsFixed
 1918   -> Bool -- ^bIsFixed
 1919   -> Bool -- ^sigmaIsFixed
 1920   -> Bool -- ^rhoIsFixed
 1921   -> Bool -- ^mIsFixed
 1922   -> Bool -- ^vegaWeighted
 1923   -> Maybe EndCriteria
 1924   -> Maybe OptimizationMethod
 1925   -> DayCounter -> IO SviInterpolatedSmileSection
 1926 sviInterpolatedSmileSection optionDate forward strikeVols hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility
 1927   a b sigma rho m aIsFixed bIsFixed sigmaIsFixed rhoIsFixed mIsFixed vegaWeighted
 1928   endCriteria method dc =
 1929   sviInterpolatedSmileSection_ optionDate forward strikes hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility vols
 1930     a b sigma rho m aIsFixed bIsFixed sigmaIsFixed rhoIsFixed mIsFixed vegaWeighted
 1931     endCriteria method dc
 1932   where (strikes, vols) = unzip (toList strikeVols)
 1933 
 1934 sviInterpolatedSmileSection_ :: (Day) -> (GenQuote q1) -- ^forward
 1935  -> ([Double]) -- ^strikes
 1936  -> (Bool) -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 1937  -> (GenQuote q2) -- ^atmVolatility
 1938  -> ([GenQuote q3]) -- ^vols
 1939  -> (Double) -- ^a
 1940  -> (Double) -- ^b
 1941  -> (Double) -- ^sigma
 1942  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1943  -> (Double) -- ^m
 1944  -> (Bool) -- ^aIsFixed
 1945  -> (Bool) -- ^bIsFixed
 1946  -> (Bool) -- ^sigmaIsFixed
 1947  -> (Bool) -- ^rhoIsFixed
 1948  -> (Bool) -- ^mIsFixed
 1949  -> (Bool) -- ^vegaWeighted
 1950  -> (Maybe EndCriteria) -> (Maybe OptimizationMethod) -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((SviInterpolatedSmileSection))
 1951 sviInterpolatedSmileSection_ a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 a17 a18 a19 a20 =
 1952   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1953   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1954   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 1955   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 1956   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
 1957   withQuoteArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 1958   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 1959   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 1960   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 1961   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 1962   let {a11' = realToFrac a11} in 
 1963   let {a12' = C2HSImp.fromBool a12} in 
 1964   let {a13' = C2HSImp.fromBool a13} in 
 1965   let {a14' = C2HSImp.fromBool a14} in 
 1966   let {a15' = C2HSImp.fromBool a15} in 
 1967   let {a16' = C2HSImp.fromBool a16} in 
 1968   let {a17' = C2HSImp.fromBool a17} in 
 1969   withMaybeEndCriteria a18 $ \a18' -> 
 1970   withMaybeOptimizationMethod a19 $ \a19' -> 
 1971   withDayCounter a20 $ \a20' -> 
 1972   preErrorCheck $ \a21' -> 
 1973   sviInterpolatedSmileSection_'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' a18' a19' a20' a21' >>= \res ->
 1974   peekSviInterpolatedSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 1975   errorCheck  a21'>>
 1976   return (res')

 2018 -- |calibrated @sigma@, see 'sviInterpolatedSmileSectionA'
 2019 sviInterpolatedSmileSectionSigma :: (SviInterpolatedSmileSection) -> IO ((Double))
 2020 sviInterpolatedSmileSectionSigma a1 =
 2021   withSviInterpolatedSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2022   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
 2023   sviInterpolatedSmileSectionSigma'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
 2024   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 2025   errorCheck  a2'>>
 2026   return (res')

 2089 
 2090 -- |a smile section calibrated to a market smile using the arbitrage-free SABR parameterization
 2091 -- (Doust's approach, via 'NoArbSabrModel' -- see 'noArbSabrSmileSection'). Unlike
 2092 -- 'noArbSabrSmileSection', which takes fixed calibrated parameters directly, this runs the
 2093 -- alpha\/beta\/nu\/rho calibration itself against the given strike\/volatility quotes -- eagerly,
 2094 -- at construction, same as 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSection'. @alpha@\/@beta@\/@nu@\/@rho@ are the
 2095 -- calibration's initial guess; @isAlphaFixed@\/@isBetaFixed@\/@isNuFixed@\/@isRhoFixed@ default to
 2096 -- 'False' upstream (unlike SVI's fixed flags, which have no default), and there is no @shift@
 2097 -- parameter (unlike 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSection').
 2098 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection :: Day -- ^optionDate
 2099   -> GenQuote q1 -- ^forward
 2100   -> NonEmpty (Double, GenQuote q3) -- ^strike/volatility quotes
 2101   -> Bool -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 2102   -> GenQuote q2 -- ^atmVolatility
 2103   -> Double -- ^alpha
 2104   -> Double -- ^beta
 2105   -> Double -- ^nu
 2106   -> Double -- ^rho
 2107   -> Bool -- ^isAlphaFixed
 2108   -> Bool -- ^isBetaFixed
 2109   -> Bool -- ^isNuFixed
 2110   -> Bool -- ^isRhoFixed
 2111   -> Bool -- ^vegaWeighted
 2112   -> Maybe EndCriteria
 2113   -> Maybe OptimizationMethod
 2114   -> DayCounter -> IO NoArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection
 2115 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection optionDate forward strikeVols hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility
 2116   alpha beta nu rho isAlphaFixed isBetaFixed isNuFixed isRhoFixed vegaWeighted
 2117   endCriteria method dc =
 2118   noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ optionDate forward strikes hasFloatingStrikes atmVolatility vols
 2119     alpha beta nu rho isAlphaFixed isBetaFixed isNuFixed isRhoFixed vegaWeighted
 2120     endCriteria method dc
 2121   where (strikes, vols) = unzip (toList strikeVols)
 2122 
 2123 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ :: (Day) -> (GenQuote q1) -- ^forward
 2124  -> ([Double]) -- ^strikes
 2125  -> (Bool) -- ^hasFloatingStrikes
 2126  -> (GenQuote q2) -- ^atmVolatility
 2127  -> ([GenQuote q3]) -- ^vols
 2128  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 2129  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 2130  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 2131  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 2132  -> (Bool) -- ^isAlphaFixed
 2133  -> (Bool) -- ^isBetaFixed
 2134  -> (Bool) -- ^isNuFixed
 2135  -> (Bool) -- ^isRhoFixed
 2136  -> (Bool) -- ^vegaWeighted
 2137  -> (Maybe EndCriteria) -> (Maybe OptimizationMethod) -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((NoArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection))
 2138 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection_ a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 a17 a18 =
 2139   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2140   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2141   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 2142   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 2143   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
 2144   withQuoteArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 2145   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 2146   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 2147   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 2148   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 2149   let {a11' = C2HSImp.fromBool a11} in 
 2150   let {a12' = C2HSImp.fromBool a12} in 
 2151   let {a13' = C2HSImp.fromBool a13} in 
 2152   let {a14' = C2HSImp.fromBool a14} in 
 2153   let {a15' = C2HSImp.fromBool a15} in 
 2154   withMaybeEndCriteria a16 $ \a16' -> 
 2155   withMaybeOptimizationMethod a17 $ \a17' -> 
 2156   withDayCounter a18 $ \a18' -> 
 2157   preErrorCheck $ \a19' -> 
 2158   noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection_'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' a18' a19' >>= \res ->
 2159   peekNoArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 2160   errorCheck  a19'>>
 2161   return (res')
 2162 
 2163 
 2164 
 2165 -- |upcast to the generic 'SmileSection' interface (e.g. for 'smileSectionVolatility'\/'smileSectionVariance').
 2166 -- A fresh-@shared_ptr@ upcast, always safe -- not the reverse (downcast) direction.
 2167 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection :: (NoArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection) -> IO ((SmileSection))
 2168 noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection a1 =
 2169   withNoArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSection a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2170   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
 2171   noArbSabrInterpolatedSmileSectionAsSmileSection'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
 2172   peekSmileSection res >>= \res' ->
 2173   errorCheck  a2'>>
 2174   return (res')

 2515 capFloorVolatilityForTime a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 2516   withGenCapFloorTermVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2517   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2518   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 2519   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 2520   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 2521   capFloorVolatilityForTime'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 2522   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 2523   errorCheck  a5'>>
 2524   return (res')

 2724 -- |Abcd-interpolated at-the-money (no-smile) volatility curve -- floating reference date,
 2725 -- floating market data. @inclusionInInterpolationFlag@ selects which quotes feed the ABCD fit
 2726 -- (upstream defaults this to all-'True' when omitted; hasquant always requires it explicitly,
 2727 -- per the widen-in-place convention for a handful of trailing defaulted params).
 2728 abcdAtmVolCurve :: Word -> Calendar
 2729   -> NonEmpty ((Word, TimeUnit), GenQuote q, Bool) -- ^tenor, volatility, inclusion flag
 2730   -> BusinessDayConvention -> DayCounter -> IO AbcdAtmVolCurve
 2731 abcdAtmVolCurve d c points bdc dc = qlAbcdAtmVolCurve d c n t qs flags bdc dc
 2732   where (ntenors, qs, flags) = unzip3 (toList points)
 2733         (n, t) = unzip ntenors
 2734 qlAbcdAtmVolCurve :: (Word) -> (Calendar) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([GenQuote q]) -> ([Bool]) -> (BusinessDayConvention) -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((AbcdAtmVolCurve))
 2735 qlAbcdAtmVolCurve a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2736   let {a1' = fromIntegral a1} in 
 2737   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2738   withIntArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 2739   withEnumArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 2740   withQuoteArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 2741   withBoolArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 2742   let {a7' = fromEnumC a7} in 
 2743   withDayCounter a8 $ \a8' -> 
 2744   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2745   qlAbcdAtmVolCurve'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2746   peekAbcdAtmVolCurve res >>= \res' ->
 2747   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2748   return (res')
 2749

 2849 abcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors :: AbcdAtmVolCurve -> IO [(Word, TimeUnit)]
 2850 abcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors o = do
 2851   (ns, us) <- qlAbcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors o
 2852   pure $ zip (map fromIntegral ns) (map toEnum us)
 2853 qlAbcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors :: (AbcdAtmVolCurve) -> IO (([Int]), ([Int]))
 2854 qlAbcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors a1 =
 2855   withAbcdAtmVolCurve a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2856   preArray $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 2857   preArray $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 2858   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 2859   qlAbcdAtmVolCurveOptionTenors'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4' >>
 2860   peekIntArray  a2'1  a2'2>>= \a2'' -> 
 2861   peekIntArray  a3'1  a3'2>>= \a3'' -> 
 2862   errorCheck  a4'>>
 2863   return (a2'', a3'')

 2910 
 2911 -- |SABR-smile 'BlackVolSurface' built from an interest-rate index, an ATM 'BlackAtmVolCurve', and
 2912 -- per-tenor ATM-rate spreads\/vol-spread matrix. @volSpreads@'s rows follow @optionTenors@ and
 2913 -- columns follow @atmRateSpreads@ (same row\/column convention as 'sabrSwaptionVolatilityCube's
 2914 -- @volSpreads@).
 2915 sabrVolSurface :: GenInterestRateIndex ix -> GenBlackAtmVolCurve b -> NonEmpty (Word, TimeUnit) -- ^optionTenors
 2916   -> NonEmpty Double -- ^atmRateSpreads
 2917   -> Matrix (GenQuote q) -- ^volSpreads
 2918   -> IO SabrVolSurface
 2919 sabrVolSurface ix atm ntenors spreads (Matrix vr vc vd) =
 2920   qlSabrVolSurface ix atm n t (toList spreads) vr vc vd
 2921   where (n, t) = unzip (toList ntenors)
 2922 qlSabrVolSurface :: (GenInterestRateIndex ix) -> (GenBlackAtmVolCurve b) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([Double]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([GenQuote q]) -> IO ((SabrVolSurface))
 2923 qlSabrVolSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2924   withInterestRateIndex a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2925   withGenBlackAtmVolCurve a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2926   withIntArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 2927   withEnumArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 2928   withDoubleArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 2929   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2930   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 2931   withQuoteArrayRaw a8 $ \a8' -> 
 2932   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2933   qlSabrVolSurface'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2934   peekSabrVolSurface res >>= \res' ->
 2935   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2936   return (res')
 2937

 2947   errorCheck  a2'>>
 2948   return (res')

 2995 -- |as 'sabrVolSurfaceIndex', converts an option tenor to its option date (folded in from
 2996 -- @InterestRateVolSurface@)
 2997 sabrVolSurfaceOptionDateFromTenor :: (SabrVolSurface) -> ((Word,TimeUnit)) -- ^optionTenor
 2998  -> IO ((Day))
 2999 sabrVolSurfaceOptionDateFromTenor a1 a2 =
 3000   withSabrVolSurface a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3001   let {(a2'1, a2'2) = fromEnumQuantity a2} in 
 3002   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 3003   sabrVolSurfaceOptionDateFromTenor'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3' >>= \res ->
 3004   let {res' = toDay res} in
 3005   errorCheck  a3'>>
 3006   return (res')

 3038 -- |A swaption vol surface behind a relinkable handle. The result /is/ a
 3039 -- 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure': pass it anywhere one is expected and everything built on it
 3040 -- keeps tracking whatever the handle currently points at, so a later 'linkSwaptionVolTo'
 3041 -- reprices already-constructed instruments without rebuilding them. Mirrors
 3042 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.relinkableYieldTermStructure'.
 3043 relinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure :: (Maybe (GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure sv)) -> IO ((RelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure))
 3044 relinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure a1 =
 3045   withMaybeSwaptionVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3046   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
 3047   relinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
 3048   peekRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure res >>= \res' ->
 3049   errorCheck  a2'>>
 3050   return (res')

 3054 -- |Point a relinkable swaption vol handle at a different surface. Everything already built on
 3055 -- the handle reprices against the new surface, with no engine rebuilt. Named distinctly from
 3056 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.linkTo' and 'linkBlackVolTo' because
 3057 -- 'BlackVolTermStructure'\/'SwaptionVolatilityStructure'\/'OptionletVolatilityStructure' all
 3058 -- live in this one module and a bare 'linkTo' per type would collide with its own siblings,
 3059 -- not just with 'Yield.chs'\/'Quote.chs'.
 3060 linkSwaptionVolTo :: (RelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure) -> (GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure sv) -> IO ()
 3061 linkSwaptionVolTo a1 a2 =
 3062   withRelinkableSwaptionVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3063   withSwaptionVolatilityStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3064   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 3065   linkSwaptionVolTo'_ a1' a2' a3' >>
 3066   errorCheck  a3'>>
 3067   return ()

 3071 -- |A constant local volatility, no time-asset dependence -- floating reference date, floating

 3075 localConstantVol' a1 a2 a3 a4 =

 3079   withDayCounter a4 $ \a4' -> 
 3080   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 3081   localConstantVol''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 3082   peekLocalVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
 3083   errorCheck  a5'>>
 3084   return (res')

 3130 capFloorTermVolCurve' :: Day -> Calendar -> BusinessDayConvention -> NonEmpty (Word, TimeUnit, GenQuote q) -> DayCounter -> IO CapFloorTermVolCurve
 3131 capFloorTermVolCurve' d c bd ntq = qlCapFloorTermVolCurve1 d c bd n t q where (n, t, q) = unzip3 (toList ntq)
 3132 qlCapFloorTermVolCurve1 :: (Day) -> (Calendar) -> (BusinessDayConvention) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([GenQuote q]) -> (DayCounter) -> IO ((CapFloorTermVolCurve))
 3133 qlCapFloorTermVolCurve1 a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 3134   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3135   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3136   let {a3' = fromEnumC a3} in 
 3137   withIntArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3138   withEnumArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 3139   withQuoteArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 3140   withDayCounter a7 $ \a7' -> 
 3141   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 3142   qlCapFloorTermVolCurve1'_ a1' a2' a3' a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 3143   peekCapFloorTermVolCurve res >>= \res' ->
 3144   errorCheck  a8'>>
 3145   return (res')

 3169 -- |A Black volatility curve built from time-dependent (ATM) market vols, interpolating on total
 3170 -- variance (linear by default, or the given 'Interpolation') -- no strike dependence; see
 3171 -- 'blackVarianceSurface' for that.
 3172 blackVarianceCurve :: Day -> NonEmpty (Day, Double) -> DayCounter -> Bool -- ^forceMonotoneVariance
 3173   -> Maybe Interpolation -> IO BlackVarianceCurve
 3174 blackVarianceCurve d dq dc f i = uncurryNested (qlBlackVarianceCurve d dd q dc f) (qlInterpolation' i) where (dd, q) = unzip (toList dq)
 3175 qlBlackVarianceCurve :: (Day) -> ([Day]) -> ([Double]) -> (DayCounter) -> (Bool) -> (Int) -> (Int) -> (Int) -> IO ((BlackVarianceCurve))
 3176 qlBlackVarianceCurve a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 3177   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3178   withDayArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3179   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 3180   withDayCounter a4 $ \a4' -> 
 3181   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 3182   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 3183   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 3184   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in 
 3185   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 3186   qlBlackVarianceCurve'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 3187   peekBlackVarianceCurve res >>= \res' ->
 3188   errorCheck  a9'>>
 3189   return (res')

 3193 -- |The @interpolator@ is applied through @BlackVarianceSurface::setInterpolation@ right after
 3194 -- construction; 'Bilinear' reproduces upstream's default. Both interpolators reproduce
 3195 -- @blackVolMatrix@ exactly at its own (date, strike) nodes -- they only differ between them.
 3196 blackVarianceSurface :: Day -> Calendar -> [Day] -- ^dates
 3197   -> [Double] -- ^strikes
 3198   -> RealMatrix -- ^blackVolMatrix
 3199   -> DayCounter
 3200   -> BlackVarianceSurfaceExtrapolation -- ^lowerExtrapolation
 3201   -> BlackVarianceSurfaceExtrapolation -- ^upperExtrapolation
 3202   -> Interpolation2D -- ^interpolator
 3203   -> IO BlackVolTermStructure
 3204 blackVarianceSurface d c ds s (RealMatrix mr mc md) = qlBlackVarianceSurface d c ds s mr mc md
 3205 qlBlackVarianceSurface :: (Day) -> (Calendar) -> ([Day]) -> ([Double]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> (RealVector) -> (DayCounter) -> (BlackVarianceSurfaceExtrapolation) -> (BlackVarianceSurfaceExtrapolation) -> (Interpolation2D) -> IO ((BlackVolTermStructure))
 3206 qlBlackVarianceSurface a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 3207   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3208   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3209   withDayArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 3210   withDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3211   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 3212   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 3213   withRealVectorRaw a7 $ \a7' -> 
 3214   withDayCounter a8 $ \a8' -> 
 3215   let {a9' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a9} in 
 3216   let {a10' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a10} in 
 3217   let {a11' = fromEnumC a11} in 
 3218   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 3219   qlBlackVarianceSurface'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 3220   peekBlackVolTermStructure res >>= \res' ->
 3221   errorCheck  a12'>>
 3222   return (res')

 3253 -- |A Black volatility surface parameterized by market deltas (put\/call deltas and, optionally,
 3254 -- an ATM quote) rather than fixed strikes -- the standard FX vol quoting convention. Constructed
 3255 -- with upstream's own defaults for the trailing options; use 'blackVolatilitySurfaceDeltaFull'
 3256 -- to override them.
 3257 blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta :: Day -> [Day] -- ^dates
 3258   -> [Double] -- ^putDeltas
 3259   -> [Double] -- ^callDeltas
 3260   -> Bool -- ^hasAtm
 3261   -> RealMatrix -- ^blackVolMatrix
 3262   -> DayCounter -> Calendar -> GenQuote q -- ^spot
 3263   -> GenYieldTermStructure y1 -- ^domesticTS
 3264   -> GenYieldTermStructure y2 -- ^foreignTS
 3265   -> IO BlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta
 3266 blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta d ds pd cd hasAtm (RealMatrix mr mc md) dc cal spot dts fts =
 3267   blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta_ d ds pd cd hasAtm mr mc md dc cal spot dts fts
 3268     Spot AtmDeltaNeutral Nothing SmileLinear False FlatVolatility (0, Days) Fwd AtmDeltaNeutral Nothing

 3270 -- |As 'blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta', but takes a 'BlackVolatilitySurfaceDeltaOpts' record for
 3271 -- the trailing options instead of hardcoding upstream's defaults.
 3272 blackVolatilitySurfaceDeltaFull :: Day -> [Day] -> [Double] -> [Double] -> Bool -> RealMatrix
 3273   -> DayCounter -> Calendar -> GenQuote q -> GenYieldTermStructure y1 -> GenYieldTermStructure y2
 3274   -> BlackVolatilitySurfaceDeltaOpts -> IO BlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta
 3275 blackVolatilitySurfaceDeltaFull d ds pd cd hasAtm (RealMatrix mr mc md) dc cal spot dts fts opts =
 3276   blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta_ d ds pd cd hasAtm mr mc md dc cal spot dts fts
 3277     (bvsdDeltaType opts) (bvsdAtmType opts) (bvsdAtmDeltaType opts)
 3278     (bvsdInterpolationMethod opts) (bvsdFlatStrikeExtrapolation opts) (bvsdTimeExtrapolationType opts)
 3279     (bvsdSwitchTenor opts) (bvsdLongTermDeltaType opts) (bvsdLongTermAtmType opts) (bvsdLongTermAtmDeltaType opts)

 3281 blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta_ :: (Day) -> ([Day]) -> ([Double]) -- ^putDeltas
 3282  -> ([Double]) -- ^callDeltas
 3283  -> (Bool) -- ^hasAtm
 3284  -> (Word) -> (Word) -> (RealVector) -- ^blackVolMatrix
 3285  -> (DayCounter) -> (Calendar) -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spot
 3286  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
 3287  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
 3288  -> (DeltaType) -- ^deltaType
 3289  -> (AtmType) -- ^atmType
 3290  -> (Maybe DeltaType) -- ^atmDeltaType
 3291  -> (SmileInterpolationMethod) -- ^interpolationMethod
 3292  -> (Bool) -- ^flatStrikeExtrapolation
 3293  -> (BlackVolTimeExtrapolationType) -- ^timeExtrapolationType
 3294  -> ((Int,TimeUnit)) -- ^switchTenor
 3295  -> (DeltaType) -- ^longTermDeltaType
 3296  -> (AtmType) -- ^longTermAtmType
 3297  -> (Maybe DeltaType) -- ^longTermAtmDeltaType
 3298  -> IO ((BlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta))
 3299 blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta_ a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 a17 a18 a19 a20 a21 a22 a23 =
 3300   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3301   withDayArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3302   withDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 3303   withDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3304   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 3305   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 3306   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 3307   withRealVectorRaw a8 $ \a8' -> 
 3308   withDayCounter a9 $ \a9' -> 
 3309   withCalendar a10 $ \a10' -> 
 3310   withQuote a11 $ \a11' -> 
 3311   withYieldTermStructure a12 $ \a12' -> 
 3312   withYieldTermStructure a13 $ \a13' -> 
 3313   let {a14' = fromEnumC a14} in 
 3314   let {a15' = fromEnumC a15} in 
 3315   let {a16' = fromMaybeEnum a16} in 
 3316   let {a17' = fromEnumC a17} in 
 3317   let {a18' = C2HSImp.fromBool a18} in 
 3318   let {a19' = fromEnumC a19} in 
 3319   let {(a20'1, a20'2) = fromEnumQuantity a20} in 
 3320   let {a21' = fromEnumC a21} in 
 3321   let {a22' = fromEnumC a22} in 
 3322   let {a23' = fromMaybeEnum a23} in 
 3323   preErrorCheck $ \a24' -> 
 3324   blackVolatilitySurfaceDelta_'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' a18' a19' a20'1  a20'2 a21' a22' a23' a24' >>= \res ->
 3325   peekBlackVolatilitySurfaceDelta res >>= \res' ->
 3326   errorCheck  a24'>>
 3327   return (res')

 3413 -- |fixed reference date, floating market data. Pass an empty 'RealMatrix'

 3416 swaptionVolatilityMatrix' :: Day -> Calendar -> BusinessDayConvention

 3418   -> [(Word, TimeUnit)] -- ^swapTenors
 3419   -> Matrix (GenQuote q) -- ^volatilities
 3420   -> DayCounter
 3421   -> Bool -- ^flatExtrapolation
 3422   -> VolatilityType
 3423   -> RealMatrix -- ^shifts
 3424   -> IO SwaptionVolatilityMatrix
 3425 swaptionVolatilityMatrix' d c bdc ot st (Matrix vr vc vd) dc' fe ty (RealMatrix sr sc sd) =
 3426   qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrix d c bdc opl opu spl spu vr vc vd dc' fe ty sr sc sd
 3427   where (opl, opu) = unzip ot; (spl, spu) = unzip st
 3428 qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrix :: (Day) -> (Calendar) -> (BusinessDayConvention) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([GenQuote q]) -> (DayCounter) -> (Bool) -> (VolatilityType) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> (RealVector) -> IO ((SwaptionVolatilityMatrix))
 3429 qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrix a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 =
 3430   withDay a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3431   withCalendar a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3432   let {a3' = fromEnumC a3} in 
 3433   withIntArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3434   withEnumArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 3435   withIntArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 3436   withEnumArray a7 $ \(a7'1, a7'2) -> 
 3437   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in 
 3438   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
 3439   withQuoteArrayRaw a10 $ \a10' -> 
 3440   withDayCounter a11 $ \a11' -> 
 3441   let {a12' = C2HSImp.fromBool a12} in 
 3442   let {a13' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a13} in 
 3443   let {a14' = fromIntegral a14} in 
 3444   let {a15' = fromIntegral a15} in 
 3445   withRealVectorRaw a16 $ \a16' -> 
 3446   preErrorCheck $ \a17' -> 
 3447   qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrix'_ a1' a2' a3' a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7'1  a7'2 a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' >>= \res ->
 3448   peekSwaptionVolatilityMatrix res >>= \res' ->
 3449   errorCheck  a17'>>
 3450   return (res')

 3498 qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrixLocate :: (SwaptionVolatilityMatrix) -> (Day) -- ^optionDate
 3499  -> ((Word,TimeUnit)) -- ^swapTenor
 3500  -> IO ((Word), (Word))
 3501 qlSwaptionVolatilityMatrixLocate a1 a2 a3 =
 3502   withSwaptionVolatilityMatrix a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3503   withDay a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3504   let {(a3'1, a3'2) = fromEnumQuantity a3} in 
 3505   alloca $ \a4' -> 
 3506   alloca $ \a5' ->

 3516 -- |A SABR-calibrated swaption volatility cube: fits a SABR smile at every (option tenor, swap
 3517 -- tenor) node from an ATM surface plus a grid of vol spreads. The result /is/ a
 3518 -- 'SwaptionVolatilityStructure' -- pass it anywhere one is expected (pricing engines,
 3519 -- 'smileSection'\/'volatilityForPeriod''\/etc.) -- but its own extra getters
 3520 -- ('sparseSabrParameters', 'denseSabrParameters', 'marketVolCube', 'volCubeAtmCalibrated',
 3521 -- 'sabrSwaptionVolatilityCubeAtmStrike'\/'\'') only accept this concrete type, not the generic one.
 3522 --
 3523 -- @endCriteria@\/@optMethod@ default to 'Nothing', which falls back to upstream's own internal
 3524 -- Levenberg-Marquardt\/EndCriteria defaults at every calibrated node. A caller-supplied
 3525 -- 'EndCriteria'\/'OptimizationMethod' is safe here since both are @shared_ptr@-boxed on the C++
 3526 -- side (see the qlaux.h comment above the QlEndCriteria\/QlOptimizationMethod typedefs) -- the
 3527 -- copy 'SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube' keeps as its own @shared_ptr@ member survives regardless of
 3528 -- when Haskell's own handle is collected, same as 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSection' and
 3529 -- 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Yield.fittedBondDiscountCurve''s fitting methods.
 3530 --
 3531 -- @volSpreads@ and @parametersGuess@ are both flattened over the (optionTenor x swapTenor)
 3532 -- product as the *outer* index (row = j*nSwapTenors+k, j over @optionTenors@, k over
 3533 -- @swapTenors@) -- not one row per @optionTenor@ the way 'swaptionVolatilityMatrix'''s grid is:
 3534 -- @matrixRows == length optionTenors * length swapTenors@ for both. @volSpreads@'s columns are
 3535 -- one per @strikeSpreads@ entry; @parametersGuess@'s columns are always exactly 4, in order
 3536 -- alpha\/beta\/nu\/rho.
 3537 --
 3538 -- Calibration is lazy: unlike 'sabrInterpolatedSmileSection', construction here does /not/ force
 3539 -- an eager fit, so this call can succeed even for inputs that will later fail to calibrate -- the
 3540 -- error only surfaces on the first 'smileSection'\/'volatilityForPeriod''\/diagnostic call.
 3541 sabrSwaptionVolatilityCube :: GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure sv -- ^atmVolStructure
 3542   -> [(Word, TimeUnit)] -- ^optionTenors
 3543   -> [(Word, TimeUnit)] -- ^swapTenors
 3544   -> [Double] -- ^strikeSpreads
 3545   -> Matrix (GenQuote q1) -- ^volSpreads
 3546   -> GenSwapIndex sidx1 -- ^swapIndexBase
 3547   -> GenSwapIndex sidx2 -- ^shortSwapIndexBase
 3548   -> Bool -- ^vegaWeightedSmileFit
 3549   -> Matrix (GenQuote q2) -- ^parametersGuess (alpha, beta, nu, rho per node)
 3550   -> Bool -- ^isAlphaFixed
 3551   -> Bool -- ^isBetaFixed
 3552   -> Bool -- ^isNuFixed
 3553   -> Bool -- ^isRhoFixed
 3554   -> Bool -- ^isAtmCalibrated: if 'True', @atmVolStructure@ must be a discrete grid structure
 3555   -- (e.g. 'swaptionVolatilityMatrix'' or another cube) -- upstream's ATM-recalibration path
 3556   -- ('denseSabrParameters'\/one branch of 'volCubeAtmCalibrated') downcasts it to
 3557   -- @SwaptionVolatilityDiscrete@ and dereferences the result unchecked, which crashes given a
 3558   -- flat 'constantSwaptionVolatility'\/'\''.
 3559   -> Maybe Double -- ^maxErrorTolerance
 3560   -> Maybe Double -- ^errorAccept
 3561   -> Bool -- ^useMaxError
 3562   -> Word -- ^maxGuesses
 3563   -> Bool -- ^backwardFlat
 3564   -> Double -- ^cutoffStrike
 3565   -> Maybe EndCriteria -- ^endCriteria
 3566   -> Maybe OptimizationMethod -- ^optMethod
 3567   -> IO SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube
 3568 sabrSwaptionVolatilityCube atm ot st ss (Matrix vr vc vd) sidx1 sidx2 vw (Matrix pr pc pd)
 3569   iaf ibf inf irf iac met eat ume mg bf cs ec om =
 3570   qlSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube atm opl opu spl spu ss vr vc vd sidx1 sidx2 vw pr pc pd
 3571     iaf ibf inf irf iac ec om met eat ume mg bf cs
 3572   where (opl, opu) = unzip ot; (spl, spu) = unzip st
 3573 qlSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube :: (GenSwaptionVolatilityStructure sv) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([Word]) -> ([TimeUnit]) -> ([Double]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([GenQuote q1]) -> (GenSwapIndex sidx1) -> (GenSwapIndex sidx2) -> (Bool) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([GenQuote q2]) -> (Bool) -> (Bool) -> (Bool) -> (Bool) -> (Bool) -> (Maybe EndCriteria) -> (Maybe OptimizationMethod) -> (Maybe Double) -> (Maybe Double) -> (Bool) -> (Word) -> (Bool) -> (Double) -> IO ((SabrSwaptionVolatilityCube))
 3574 qlSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 a17 a18 a19 a20 a21 a22 a23 a24 a25 a26 a27 a28 =
 3575   withSwaptionVolatilityStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3576   withIntArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3577   withEnumArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 3578   withIntArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3579   withEnumArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 3580   withDoubleArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 3581   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 3582   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in 
 3583   withQuoteArrayRaw a9 $ \a9' -> 
 3584   withSwapIndex a10 $ \a10' -> 
 3585   withSwapIndex a11 $ \a11' -> 
 3586   let {a12' = C2HSImp.fromBool a12} in 
 3587   let {a13' = fromIntegral a13} in 
 3588   let {a14' = fromIntegral a14} in 
 3589   withQuoteArrayRaw a15 $ \a15' -> 
 3590   let {a16' = C2HSImp.fromBool a16} in 
 3591   let {a17' = C2HSImp.fromBool a17} in 
 3592   let {a18' = C2HSImp.fromBool a18} in 
 3593   let {a19' = C2HSImp.fromBool a19} in 
 3594   let {a20' = C2HSImp.fromBool a20} in 
 3595   withMaybeEndCriteria a21 $ \a21' -> 
 3596   withMaybeOptimizationMethod a22 $ \a22' -> 
 3597   let {a23' = fromMaybeDouble a23} in 
 3598   let {a24' = fromMaybeDouble a24} in 
 3599   let {a25' = C2HSImp.fromBool a25} in 
 3600   let {a26' = fromIntegral a26} in 
 3601   let {a27' = C2HSImp.fromBool a27} in 
 3602   let {a28' = realToFrac a28} in 
 3603   preErrorCheck $ \a29' -> 
 3604   qlSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' a15' a16' a17' a18' a19' a20' a21' a22' a23' a24' a25' a26' a27' a28' a29' >>= \res ->
 3605   peekSabrSwaptionVolatilityCube res >>= \res' ->
 3606   errorCheck  a29'>>
 3607   return (res')

 3700   where (opl, opu) = unzip ot; (spl, spu) = unzip st

 3705   withEnumArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 3706   withIntArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 3707   withEnumArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 3708   withDoubleArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
 3709   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 3710   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in

 3723 -- |The interpolated volatility-spread matrix (option tenor x swap tenor) at the given strike
 3724 -- index (0-based, into the @strikeSpreads@ the cube was built with).

 3727 qlInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCubeVolSpreads :: (InterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube) -> (Word) -- ^i

 3730   withInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCube a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3731   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 3732   prePtr $ \a3' -> 
 3733   prePtr $ \a4' -> 
 3734   preArray $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 3735   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 3736   qlInterpolatedSwaptionVolatilityCubeVolSpreads'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5'1  a5'2 a6' >>

 3738   peekWord  a4'>>= \a4'' -> 
 3739   peekRealVector  a5'1  a5'2>>= \a5'' -> 
 3740   errorCheck  a6'>>
 3741   return (a3'', a4'', a5'')

 3767 -- |Per-node calibrated SABR parameters, meaningfully populated only when the cube was built with
